TL;DR: 本文研究LLM-as-judge评估中“评审模型更换”导致分数变化的测量有效性问题，发现不同升级路径并不等价，需更完整的偏差与审计报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当候选回答不变时，仅更换评审LLM就可能导致评分变化，这会削弱LLM-as-judge结果的可比性与测量有效性。作者希望厘清评审器升级是否稳定提升评估质量，以及升级后偏差是否被缓解。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上比较两类现实可用的评审器升级路径：Qwen3 dense judge从1.7B扩展到32B参数，以及MiniMax M2到M2.7的API版本迁移；同时分析位置偏差、冗长偏差、重复采样jury中错误相关性，以及结构化辩论协议对判决的影响，并强调解析器与fallback日志的重要性。

Result: 评审器升级并非可互换：只有Qwen3 1.7B到4B显示出稳健的相邻增益，而MiniMax相邻版本发布并未表现出类似稳定收益。更强评审器能降低但不能消除位置和冗长偏差。重复采样jury在错误高度相关时收益有限。结构化辩论能显著改变决策，但若缺少解析器和fallback日志，无法确认变化源自真正的审议过程。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge评估报告应将评审器更换视为测量有效性风险，并系统报告数据集切片、偏差探针、错误依赖估计和协议审计轨迹，以提升结果可解释性、可复现性和跨版本可比性。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [110] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAESTRO，一种面向 MoE 语言模型的结构化专家剪枝框架，通过将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，并利用平稳分布衡量全局专家重要性，在 50% 压缩下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活 MoE 模型推理时每个 token 只激活少量参数，但完整专家库仍需常驻内存，导致部署成本高。现有结构化剪枝多面向稠密 Transformer，依赖局部启发式重要性评估，难以捕捉 MoE 路由中跨层、跨专家的依赖关系。

Method: MAESTRO 将自回归生成过程中的专家激活序列近似为遍历马尔可夫链，用转移关系刻画专家路由依赖，并通过马尔可夫链的平稳分布得到具有全局感知能力的专家重要性分数，再据此进行结构化专家剪枝。

Result: 在五个不同领域评测中，包括 Safety、Bias 和 Ethics 等任务，MAESTRO 在严格 50% 压缩设置下，相比最强基线平均性能保持率最高提升 10.61%，同时跨任务方差显著更低。

Conclusion: 考虑 MoE 路由全局依赖的剪枝策略比局部启发式更适合专家稀疏化；MAESTRO 能在大幅降低专家库规模的同时更稳定地保持模型性能，并提升跨异质任务的泛化一致性。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [111] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree 是一种免训练的最佳优先草稿树方法，用 Domino 的路径依赖条件校正来给树节点打分，在多项基准上提升了 LLM speculative decoding 的接受长度与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 block-diffusion drafter（如 DFlash）一次生成草稿块但只建模各位置边缘分布；DDTree 等最佳优先树方法依赖因子化边缘分布，无法表达 Domino 中 GRU 因果校正带来的路径依赖、非因子化 token 分布。因此需要一种能利用 Domino 条件校正结构的树搜索解码方法。

Method: 提出 DominoTree：训练-free 的 best-first draft tree。它沿每条 root-to-node 路径使用 Domino 的条件、非因子化校正分数评估候选节点；为降低计算开销，将每个节点的校正限制在 top-M 候选上。同时实现 GPU-native、CUDA graph 的树构建器，并保证与 Python 参考实现 bit-identical，从而不改变接受行为。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 的八个 benchmark 上，相比自回归解码最高达到 6.6x 加速；在所有测试温度下获得最高平均接受长度，最高每轮 10.7 tokens。默认 CUDA 构建器下，相比 released Domino decoder 在所有温度上吞吐更高，Qwen3-4B 总体提升 9–10%，Alpaca 上最高 +22%；也优于 DDTree/CaDDTree。Qwen3-8B 上在所有温度保持最高接受长度，T=0 时吞吐比 DDTree 高 +24%；高温下相对 DDTree/CaDDTree 的吞吐优势缩小为持平或小幅落后，但总体仍优于 DFlash 和 Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree 将 Domino 的路径依赖校正有效融入最佳优先树搜索，在不额外训练的情况下提高 speculative decoding 的草稿质量和整体吞吐；其 GPU 原生实现使树构建开销可控，是对 Domino、DFlash、DDTree/CaDDTree 的实用改进。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [112] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX 是一个面向大规模预训练语料的函数调用式数据精炼框架，通过删除、修改、插入三类操作实现细粒度实例级编辑，并在多语料实验中取得最佳平均表现与更高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可用训练数据接近物理上限，单纯依赖 Scaling Laws 扩展数据带来的收益递减。现有大规模语料精炼方法存在质量、效率和可靠性瓶颈：规则方法难适应实例差异，LLM 方法质量较高但难以满足大规模处理的效率与稳定性需求。

Method: 论文提出 UltraX：首先通过数据集自适应提示优化，引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本；随后利用行对齐映射与动态上下文替换，将原文—精炼文本对转换为结构化程序监督。训练阶段通过低置信样本过滤与按操作组合比例控制采样提升监督质量并稳定分布；推理执行阶段采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统化后处理，对模型输出进行规范化与校验。

Result: 实验表明，UltraX 在所有语料上的平均性能最高，并且在使用更少训练 tokens 的情况下也能达到或超过基线方法，体现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过补全编辑操作空间、构建可靠的程序监督生成流程，并增强推理执行阶段的稳定性，为大规模预训练数据精炼提供了一种兼顾质量、效率与可靠性的有效方案。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [113] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm 是一种用于 LLM 网页搜索代理的渐进式递归委派框架，通过动态生成搜索节点、递归分解任务并进行证据驱动的协作扩展，在多类深度与广度搜索任务上优于单代理和现有多代理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 风格单代理受限于单条长轨迹与有限上下文，难以同时兼顾搜索深度和覆盖广度；现有多代理方法虽能并行提升覆盖率，但在递归深挖、协作适应性和基于证据的任务扩展方面仍不足。

Method: 论文提出 WebSwarm：在推理过程中联合构建任务分解、递归扩展和代理协作。系统动态实例化 agentic search nodes，每个节点包含局部目标与搜索模式；节点可自行解决任务或继续委派子节点，并将证据与结果向上传递。框架还会先探测网络上任务相关信息的组织方式，以指导节点扩展，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch 和 GISA 等基准上，WebSwarm 在深度搜索、广度搜索以及深广交织任务中均稳定超过单代理和多代理基线。消融、任务难度、网页工具效率和模型泛化分析进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: WebSwarm 表明，面向复杂网页研究任务，动态递归委派、证据驱动扩展和可复用协作经验能够显著提升 LLM 搜索代理的深度、广度和适应性，为多代理搜索系统设计提供了有价值的方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [114] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究用于强化学习奖励信号的LLM评审器在引用质量评估中的校准问题，发现便宜模型可达到竞争性表现，但不同评审器存在显著方向性偏差，需在用于奖励前校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习越来越依赖LLM judge按rubric打分并充当奖励模型；在深度研究系统的引用质量任务中，评审器的能力与偏差会直接影响训练方向，因此必须评估其可靠性与校准需求。

Method: 作者构建/使用一个对抗性长文本基准，针对搜索增强LLM生成内容中的 attribution-citation 对，在source relevance和factual support两个维度上评估8个来自3个模型家族的现成LLM judge，并与人工复核金标对比；共1,248个rubric决策，其中378个困难样本来自judge分歧后的裁决。

Result: 较便宜的judge在两个维度上仍具竞争力；GPT-5-mini在source relevance的pass-class F1最高，为0.908，κ=0.636；在factual support上，各judge置信区间重叠，统计上难以区分，没有单一模型占优。但在相近F1下，不同judge的通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率差异明显。

Conclusion: 仅用标量F1会掩盖评审器的方向性偏差，而这种偏差会被下游强化学习过程放大或固化；因此，将引用rubric用作奖励信号前必须先校准judge，而且不一定需要使用最昂贵的模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [115] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文检验葡萄牙国家语言模型 AMALIA-9B 在“权威”道德基础编码任务中是否不仅与人工编码一致，而且真正遵循理论构念；结果显示其分解式理论规则表现显著下降，说明存在效度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型可为本语言共同体提供本土化测量工具，但仅凭与人工编码的一致性无法证明其对理论构念的有效测量。作者关注模型是否按构念理论进行推断，还是依赖表面相关特征获得看似正确的标签。

Method: 提出并使用“恢复差距”检验：将整体提示拆解为代码本中的原子条款，再按理论规则重组，比较分解式流程与整体提示的性能差异。研究考察经校准的英文测量工具是否能迁移到 AMALIA-9B 与欧洲葡萄牙语语料，并与一个开放多语种大模型在同一葡萄牙语语料和指令下对比。

Result: AMALIA-9B 在整体提示下与人工编码接近，F1 仅比大得多的开放模型低约六个百分点；但分解式方法只恢复约一半的整体性能。错误分析显示模型可能依赖表面线索，尤其是靠近权威人物的道德愤慨表达。相比之下，开放多语种模型在相同葡萄牙语语料和指令下能缩小恢复差距。

Conclusion: AMALIA-9B 可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚不能独立可靠地测量该理论构念。该研究只是单一反例，并非否定国家语言模型整体价值；它强调主权/国家大模型评测不仅应考察与人工编码的一致性，还应检验这种一致性是否由符合理论的证据路径支持。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [116] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench提出了一个面向主动智能体的能力驱动基准，用于在动态真实环境中评估其多轮交互、工具使用与跨平台协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测多依赖沙盒环境、单轮评估和场景式任务分类，难以反映真实动态环境中的表现，也难以定位失败背后的具体能力短板。

Method: 作者围绕五类基础能力构建评测体系：技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解和跨平台协调；设计400个双语真实任务，并在实时Docker容器中通过细粒度逐步检查点评分。评测采用执行者智能体、隐藏监督者智能体和用户智能体组成的闭环策略，以模拟多轮人类反馈且避免泄露评分标准；同时在多个智能体框架下评估不同前沿模型。

Result: 实验比较显示，基础模型能力与智能体框架设计共同影响真实环境任务表现；该基准能够更细粒度地区分不同模型和框架在各能力维度上的优劣。

Conclusion: UniClawBench为主动智能体提供了更贴近真实应用、更可诊断的评测方案，有助于推动模型能力分析、智能体框架优化和未来真实环境智能体研究。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Smoothing Exponents and Decoupling in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07997)
*Zhiwen Lin,Hongsen Qiu,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究半有限 von Neumann 代数中 max-relative entropy 的平滑指数，并给出精确指数公式；进一步将结果用于带半有限参考系统的催化量子信息解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维量子信息理论中关于平滑 max-relative entropy 与解耦可靠性指数的结果依赖矩阵维数、谱可数性等工具；在半有限 von Neumann 代数框架下，需要建立不依赖维数估计的内禀理论。

Method: 作者发展了替代有限维维数依赖论证的算子代数工具，证明 von Neumann 代数版本的内禀 layer-cake 引理，并移除了有限维证明中的可数谱假设，从而推广到半有限情形。

Result: 得到半有限 von Neumann 代数中平滑 max-relative entropy 的精确指数公式；证明解耦可靠性指数仍由与有限维理论相同的 sandwiched Rényi mutual information 公式刻画。

Conclusion: 平滑指数的本质由底层 von Neumann 代数结构决定，而非矩阵维数估计；相关量子信息解耦指数理论可自然推广到半有限 von Neumann 代数参考系统。

Abstract: We study the smoothing exponent of the max-relative entropy in semifinite von Neumann algebras. Our main result gives an exact exponent formula in this setting. The proof develops operator-algebraic replacements for the dimension-dependent tools used in finite-dimensional arguments. These ingredients show that the smoothing exponent is governed by the underlying von Neumann algebraic structure rather than by matrix dimension estimates. As an application, we formulate catalytic quantum information decoupling with a semifinite von Neumann algebraic reference system. We prove an intrinsic layer-cake lemma for von Neumann algebras, which removes the countable spectrum assumption in the finite-dimensional proof and yields the corresponding semifinite estimate. Consequently, the decoupling reliability exponent is described by the same sandwiched Rényi mutual information formula as in the finite-dimensional theory.

</details>


### [118] [RadioDiff-v2: Generative Angular Radio Maps for Multi-Beam Selection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08045)
*Xiucheng Wang,Junxi Huang,Nan Cheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 RadioDiff-v2：一种用于从几何信息预测角功率谱（APS）的双分支一维扩散 Transformer，在零样本环境中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G 网络中的角无线电图对波束选择和接收机定位至关重要，但在 NLOS 条件下几何到 APS 的映射病态且多解；传统最小失真回归器输出条件均值，容易过度平滑并丢失多径结构。

Method: 作者将任务建模为感知-失真权衡问题，提出基于 flow matching 训练的双分支一维扩散 Transformer，结合周期角度编码、自适应层归一化条件注入、傅里叶角度混合器，以及联合速度头和干净信号头；同时用每指标估计器组合从单一模型中支持分布采样、点估计、波束选择与条件似然定位。

Result: 在 99 个未见环境、100 万条链路的零样本测试中，RadioDiff-v2 在所有指标上领先基线：Wasserstein-1 距离为 0.39 dB，逐 bin 误差低于回归基线，八波束 NLOS 扫描损失为 2.43 dB，四基站定位误差为 20.6 像素。

Conclusion: RadioDiff-v2 通过扩散生成与确定性传输归纳偏置兼顾分布建模和高质量点估计，可更好保留多径结构，并统一服务于 6G 中的 APS 预测、波束选择和定位任务。

Abstract: Angular radio maps describe the received-power distribution over the angle of arrival and underpin beam selection and receiver localization in sixth-generation (6G) networks. Predicting the angular power spectrum (APS) from geometry is difficult, because the mapping is ill-posed in non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions and must generalize to unseen environments. Distortion-minimizing regressors return the conditional mean, which over-smooths the spectrum and erases the multipath structure that downstream tasks need. We cast the task as a perception-distortion problem and propose RadioDiff-v2, a dual-branch one-dimensional diffusion transformer trained with flow matching. It couples periodic angular encoding, adaptive layer-normalization conditioning, a Fourier angular mixer, and joint velocity and clean-signal heads. A per-metric estimator portfolio reads every deployment quantity from this single model, so that samples carry the distribution, the clean-signal head supplies a regression-grade point estimate, Bayes-optimal rules select beams, and the conditional likelihood localizes the receiver. We prove that a concentrated conditional yields a straight probability-flow trajectory that one step integrates exactly, identifying deterministic transport as the correct inductive bias. On a zero-shot test of 99 environments and one million links, RadioDiff-v2 leads every baseline on every metric, with a 0.39 dB Wasserstein-1 distance, per-bin error below the regression baseline, a 2.43 dB eight-beam NLOS sweep loss, and a 20.6-pixel localization error with four base stations. Code is available at https://github.com/UNIC-Lab/RadioDiff-v2.

</details>


### [119] [Generalization Theory for Through-the-Wall Radar Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08144)
*Weicheng Gao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于穿墙雷达人类活动识别（TWR HAR）的泛化分析框架，从理论上解释目标域误差来源，并分析跨人、跨视角、跨墙体分布偏移对模型泛化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TWR HAR 在非视距室内感知等场景中具有应用价值，但人员差异、观测视角差异和墙体条件差异会造成结构化分布偏移，显著削弱识别模型在目标域上的泛化能力；现有研究缺乏对目标域误差来源的严格理论解释。

Method: 论文建立了统一的源域到目标域学习表述，建模室内人体运动学、TWR 回波生成、雷达图像形成、特征表示以及有界权重神经网络；定义源风险、目标风险、经验风险和可容许物理域描述符，并推导统一的目标域泛化界；进一步将结构化偏移项分解为跨人、跨视角和跨墙体分量，分析物理低维表示、多源训练和参数空间覆盖对收紧泛化界的作用。

Result: 仿真与实测实验共同支持理论分析，表明所提出的泛化界能够解释不同结构化域偏移下的目标域误差变化，并展示了理论框架对实际 TWR HAR 模型设计与评估的指导价值。

Conclusion: 该工作为 TWR HAR 的跨域泛化提供了系统理论框架，明确了目标域误差的物理与学习因素来源，并指出低维物理特征、多源数据和充分参数覆盖是提升泛化性能的重要途径。

Abstract: Through-the-wall radar (TWR) human activity recognition (HAR) is important for non-line-of-sight indoor sensing, security monitoring, and emergency rescue. However, structured distribution shifts caused by person variation, observation-view variation, and wall-condition variation severely degrade recognition generalization, while the origin of the target-domain error still lacks a rigorous theoretical explanation. To address this issue, a generalization-analysis framework for TWR HAR is proposed in this paper. First, models for indoor human kinematics, TWR echo generation, radar image formation, feature representation, and bounded-weight neural networks are established within a unified source-to-target learning formulation. Then, the source risk, target risk, empirical risk, and admissible physical domain descriptor are defined, and a unified target-domain generalization bound is derived. Next, the structured shift term is decomposed into cross-person, cross-view, and cross-wall components, and the bound-tightening effects of physical low-dimensional representations, multi-source training, and parameter-space coverage are analyzed. Simulated and measured experiments jointly support the resulting theoretical analysis and illustrate its application value.

</details>


### [120] [Multiuser Zak-OTFS on the Uplink with Superimposed Spread-Pilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08247)
*Sai Pradeep Muppaneni,Ananthanarayanan Chockalingam*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究多用户 Zak-OTFS 上行系统中异构 DD 周期/帧长用户的接入与 IOR 估计，提出基于 TF 平移的多址与叠加扩频导频估计框架，仿真显示在 sinc 脉冲下可接近单用户性能并提升频谱效率，但 Gaussian 脉冲下嵌入式导频更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户 Zak-OTFS 系统中，不同用户可能具有异构 delay-Doppler 周期或帧大小，如何在降低用户间干扰的同时进行高效、准确的输入输出关系（IOR）/信道估计，是提升系统可靠性和频谱效率的关键问题。

Method: 通过时频平移将多个用户放置在 TF 平面互不重叠区域，实现多址接入；分别针对 sinc 与 Gaussian 成形脉冲推导各用户到基站的有效 DD 域信道闭式表达；利用叠加扩频导频进行 IOR 估计，其中扩频导频由重塑后的 Zadoff-Chu 序列经 FFT 得到；采用基于 DD 字典的迭代估计与数据检测方法，以缓解叠加导频带来的导频-数据干扰。

Result: TF 平移多址下用户间干扰可忽略，多用户 IOR 估计可解耦为多个独立单用户估计问题；仿真表明该方法的 NMSE 和 BER 接近对应单用户系统。sinc 脉冲下，叠加扩频导频相比嵌入式导频在较宽的用户功率比范围内具有更高频谱效率；Gaussian 脉冲下，由于残余 IUI 与显著导频-数据干扰，嵌入式导频频谱效率更高。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于 TF 平移的多用户 Zak-OTFS 接入可有效抑制 IUI，并使多用户估计问题近似单用户化；所提出的叠加扩频导频与 DD 字典迭代估计框架在 sinc 脉冲下尤其有效，同时对信道功率时延分布和最大 Doppler 偏移变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the uplink of a multiuser Zak-OTFS system comprising users with heterogeneous delay-Doppler (DD) periods/frame sizes. Multiple access is achieved through time-frequency (TF) shifts that place the users in non-overlapping regions of the TF plane. Closed-form expressions for the effective DD domain channel between each user and the base station are derived for sinc and Gaussian pulse shaping filters. The inter-user interference (IUI) is shown to be negligible under the TF-shift-based multiple access, thereby decoupling the multiuser input-output relation (IOR) estimation problem into independent single-user estimation problems. For IOR estimation, a superimposed spread-pilot framework is employed. The spread-pilot sequence is obtained by applying FFT to a reshaped Zadoff-Chu sequence. To mitigate the pilot-data interference introduced by the superimposed spread-pilot, a DD dictionary-based IOR estimation scheme that iterates between IOR estimation and data detection is employed. Simulation results for a multiuser Zak-OTFS system demonstrate that the resulting IOR estimates achieve normalized mean-square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER) performances that closely match those of the corresponding single-user system. Furthermore, for sinc pulse shaping, the superimposed spread-pilot frame achieves higher spectral-efficiency compared to embedded pilot frame across a wide range of inter-user power ratios. For Gaussian pulse shaping, however, the embedded pilot frame achieves a higher spectral efficiency due to the combined effects of residual IUI and significant pilot-data interference in the case of superimposed spread-pilot. The robustness of the estimation framework to variations in channel power-delay profile and maximum Doppler shift is also demonstrated.

</details>


### [121] [Deep Reinforcement Learning-Empowered Wireless Sensor Networking for 6G Closed-Loop Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08272)
*Chengleyang Lei,Wei Feng,Yunfei Chen,Yongxu Zhu,Ning Ge,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人在远程/危险环境中依赖传感器与边缘信息中心构成SC3闭环时，如何通过传感器到边缘信息中心的带宽分配来降低LQR控制成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个机器人感知与计算能力有限，需要外部传感器和边缘信息中心支持状态获取、数据分析与控制生成；通信带宽受限会引入信息失真并影响闭环控制性能，因此需要面向控制目标优化通信资源。

Method: 基于互信息理论建模有限通信速率导致的失真噪声；在卡尔曼滤波与LQR控制策略下，将控制过程建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）；采用基于深度强化学习的传感器到EIH带宽分配方案，并使用PPO算法训练智能体。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的DRL带宽分配方案相比基线方法具有更优的闭环控制性能，能够降低LQR控制成本。

Conclusion: 该工作说明在SC3闭环系统中，通信带宽分配应与控制性能联合优化；基于POMDP建模和PPO训练的DRL方法可有效处理受限通信下的传感器带宽分配问题。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly deployed in remote or hazardous areas for mission-critical control tasks. Due to their limited individual capabilities, they have to rely on other field sensors to obtain the state information of targets, and also a dedicated edge information hub (EIH) to enable information exchange, sensing data analysis and control command generation. Such configuration follows a sensing-communication-computing-control (SC3) closed loop. To optimize the whole closed-loop performance, this paper minimizes the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) control cost by designing the sensor-to-EIH bandwidth allocation. Specifically, we first model the distortion noise caused by limited communication data rate based on the mutual information theory. Next, under the control policy based on the Kalman filter and LQR controller, we formulate the control process as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), and develop a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based sensor-to-EIH bandwidth allocation scheme. The proximal policy optimization (PPO) algorithm is utilized to train the DRL agent. Simulation results are provided to show the superiority of the proposed DRL-based scheme.

</details>


### [122] [A Study Of Skew-Polycyclic Codes Over A Non-Chain Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08304)
*Seema Antil,Seema Chahal,Manju Khan,Sugandha Maheshwary*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限非链环 R_{u^2,v^2,p^m} 上的斜多循环码与斜常循环码，给出结构刻画、自由码判定与秩计算，并通过分解商环将一般长度问题化约到中心不可约因子对应的左理想研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于有限链环或较简单环（如 F_{p^m}+uF_{p^m}）上常循环码的结果，但对有限非链环 R_{u^2,v^2,p^m} 上、尤其在自同构诱导的斜多项式环中的斜多循环码和斜常循环码结构尚需系统推广。

Method: 在斜多项式环 R[x;Θ] 中，以中心多项式 f(x)^j 关联的商环为对象，描述其左理想对应的斜多循环码；在中心性条件下分解 x^{np^s}-λ 对应的商环，将斜 (λ,Θ)-常循环码分解为若干由中心不可约因子 f(x)^j 控制的子问题；并分别讨论长度 p^s 及 Θ 为恒等时的常循环情形。

Result: 论文刻画了斜多循环码的结构，给出了自由斜多循环码的判定及其秩；建立了 x^{np^s}-λ 商环的分解框架；将长度 np^s 的斜常循环码研究归约为若干左理想分类问题；并通过实例得到若干最优码。

Conclusion: 该工作把已有关于 F_{p^m}+uF_{p^m} 上常循环码的结果推广到更复杂的有限非链环 R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}，并为非链环上斜常循环码与常循环码的结构分析提供了统一代数框架。

Abstract: For a prime \(p\) and a positive integer \(m\), let \(\mathbb{F}_{p^m}\) be the finite field of cardinality \(p^m\), and let
  $
  R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}
  =\mathbb{F}_{p^m}+u\mathbb{F}_{p^m}+v\mathbb{F}_{p^m}
  +uv\mathbb{F}_{p^m},
  ~ u^2=v^2=0,\ uv=vu,
  $
  be a finite non-chain ring. In this paper, we study skew polycyclic codes of length \(lj\) associated with \(f(x)^j\), where \(f(x)\) is a central polynomial of degree \(l\) in $R_{u^2, v^2, p^m}[x; Θ],$ where $Θ$ being an automorphism of \(R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}\). We describe these codes, characterize free skew polycyclic codes, and determine their ranks.
  Under suitable centrality assumptions, we decompose the quotient ring associated with \(x^{np^s}-λ\), where \(\gcd(n,p)=1\) and \(Θ(λ)=λ\). This reduces the study of skew \((λ,Θ)\)-constacyclic codes of length \(np^s\) to the study of left ideals of
  $\frac{R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}[x;Θ]}{\langle f(x)^j\rangle},
  $ where \(f(x)\) is a central irreducible divisor of degree \(l\) of \(x^{np^s}-λ\), for an invertible element \(λ\in R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}\) and \(j\in\mathbb{N}\).
  We then apply these results to skew \((λ,Θ)\)-constacyclic codes of length \(p^s\) for different classes of units \(λ\). Several examples are presented to illustrate the theory and to obtain optimal codes. Finally, when \(Θ\) is the identity automorphism, we study constacyclic codes of length \(np^s\) over \(R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}\), according as \(x^n-α_0\) is irreducible or reducible over \(\mathbb{F}_{p^m}\). These results extend the work of \cite{CCDF18} and \cite{ZTG18} on constacyclic codes of length \(np^s\) over \(\mathbb{F}_{p^m}+u\mathbb{F}_{p^m}\) to the finite non-chain ring \(R_{u^2,v^2,p^m}\).

</details>


### [123] [Why Constants Matter in Distribution Testing: From Uniformity to Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08378)
*Alon Kipnis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文强调分布拟合优度检验中“尖锐常数”的重要性：在样本复杂度阶数相同的检验之间，常数决定实际风险、功效与调参效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布检验理论多关注率级别样本复杂度，即样本量如何随字母表大小、与原假设的距离和目标错误概率缩放；但在多个检验同为率最优时，仍无法回答哪个检验在实际风险或功效上更优。

Method: 作者提出从常数级别分析分布检验，并将尖锐常数类比为参数估计中的 Fisher 信息和非参数估计中的 Pinsker 常数；以大字母表均匀性检验为例，说明常数如何刻画有效信噪比，并进一步讨论其在校准检验分箱数选择中的作用。

Result: 尖锐常数能够区分同阶最优但功效不同的检验，揭示检验问题的有效信噪比，并为下游应用中的调参提供依据。

Conclusion: 分布检验不应只停留在样本复杂度阶数层面；常数级别理论对于实际检验设计、功效比较和应用调参具有核心意义。

Abstract: Distribution goodness-of-fit testing has developed a powerful rate-level theory: we often know how the required sample size scales with the alphabet size, the separation from the null, and the target error probability. Uniformity testing is the canonical example. One can distinguish the uniform distribution on $N$ categories from alternatives at total-variation distance at least $ε$ with far fewer than $N$ samples, and the optimal scaling is now well understood.
  But rate-level theory leaves an important question unresolved: among several tests with the same sample-complexity order, which one actually gives the best risk or power? This is a constant-level question. It is especially relevant in modern applications where distribution testing is used not merely as an asymptotic abstraction, but as a practical design tool.
  This note argues that sharp constants in distribution testing play a role analogous to Fisher information in parametric estimation and Pinsker's constant in nonparametric estimation. First, they distinguish between tests that are all rate-optimal but not equally powerful. Second, they reveal the effective signal-to-noise ratio governing the testing problem. Third, they can guide tuning-parameter choices in downstream applications. We illustrate this perspective through large-alphabet uniformity testing and then explain why the same logic matters for choosing the number of bins in calibration testing.

</details>


### [124] [Optimization and Deep Learning based Resource Allocation for UAV-Aided Wireless Communication with Rotatable Antenna Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08420)
*Fengcheng Pei,Lin Xiang,Anja Klein,Robert Schober*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究在多天线无人机辅助通信中引入可旋转天线阵列，通过联合优化阵列朝向与波束成形提升多用户和速率并保障QoS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定天线阵列在无人机通信中空间自由度受限，难以充分对准用户高增益方向并降低用户间信道相关性；可旋转天线阵列可通过三维姿态调整提供额外空间自由度，从而改善传输质量和干扰管理。

Method: 作者建立以最大化多用户和速率为目标、满足单用户QoS约束的联合优化问题，变量包括RAA朝向和波束成形。针对问题非凸且变量强耦合的特点，提出基于惩罚对偶分解（PDD）的迭代优化框架；同时为降低实时计算复杂度，进一步提出基于图神经网络（GNN）的深度学习框架以近似高质量解。

Result: 仿真表明，可旋转天线阵列相比固定天线阵列在无人机辅助通信中具有显著性能优势；PDD优化框架能够高可靠性地满足严格QoS约束并获得较好和速率；GNN框架在大幅降低计算时间的同时取得接近优化方法的和速率性能，并对用户位置信息误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAA为多天线无人机通信提供了有效的额外空间自由度，可显著提升多用户传输与干扰管理能力；结合PDD优化与GNN快速推理，可在性能、QoS可靠性和实时部署复杂度之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Multi-antenna unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-aided communication presents a promising solution to increase the system capacity and improve the quality of service (QoS) of the future wireless networks. In this paper, we equip a UAV platform with a rotatable antenna array (RAA), which can be rotated flexibly in three-dimensional (3D) space via an onboard gimbal, enabling additional spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs) for improving multiuser signal transmission and interference management. Compared with a conventional fixed antenna array (FAA), the RAA can proactively align users with the high-gain region of its antenna elements and reduce the spatial channel correlations among users. To demonstrate the advantages of RAA, we jointly design the RAA orientation and beamforming to maximize the sum-rate of multiple users subject to per-user QoS constraints. The formulated problem is highly nonconvex and exhibits strong coupling between the RAA orientation and beamforming variables. To solve this challenging problem, we propose first an optimization framework based on the penalty dual decomposition (PDD) method to iteratively optimize RAA orientation and beamforming. While the optimization framework yields high reliability in QoS satisfaction and favorable sum-rate performance, its iterative nature may hinder real-time deployment. To accelerate the joint design and preserve a high-quality solution, we further propose a deep learning (DL) framework based on graph neural networks (GNNs). Simulation results demonstrate that RAAs significantly outperform FAAs in UAV-aided communication. Additionally, the proposed optimization framework is capable of satisfying stringent QoS requirements with high reliability, while the proposed DL framework attains comparable sum-rate performance with substantially reduced computation time and exhibits robustness to user position information errors.

</details>


### [125] [Learning LDPC codes with quantized density evolution over relaxed protographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08484)
*Gennady Shutkov,Dmitry Artemasov,Alexey Frolov,Pavel Rybin,Kirill Andreev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向长码长原图 LDPC 码的确定性梯度下降设计框架，直接在松弛原图上优化，并用密度演化 BER 作为可微损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDPC 校验矩阵设计是困难的组合优化问题；传统启发式搜索需调参且计算昂贵，现有 decoder-in-the-loop 梯度方法依赖噪声蒙特卡洛估计、软矩阵线搜索，且对长码代价较高。

Method: 将原图每个元素解释为对应二元元素取 1 的概率，构造可直接作用于松弛原图的密度演化 BER 损失；证明该松弛密度演化等价于相关二元原图集合的平均密度演化性能，并采用标准梯度下降自主优化。

Result: 在 min-sum 译码器上的数值实验表明，优化得到的原图在相同原图维度下优于 5G LDPC 码。

Conclusion: 确定性 DE 与信息量较高的梯度使方法收敛快速可靠，适合长原图 LDPC 码的自动化设计，并能取得优于现有标准码的性能。

Abstract: We consider the design of low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes for a given iterative decoder. Despite tools such as direct simulation, density evolution (DE), and EXIT-chart analysis, selecting a parity-check matrix remains a difficult combinatorial optimization problem. Existing approaches often rely on population-based search, random mutations, genetic algorithms, or related heuristics, which require careful parameter tuning and may be computationally expensive. Recent gradient descent (GD)-based methods optimize relaxed parity-check matrices by differentiating through decoder simulations. However, such decoder-in-the-loop strategies rely on noisy Monte Carlo estimates, require line search over soft matrix representations, and remain costly for long LDPC codes. Moreover, although optimization is performed in a relaxed domain, the loss is typically evaluated only at integer-valued parity-check matrices.
  In this work, we focus on the design of long protograph-based LDPC codes and propose a deterministic GD-based framework that operates directly on a relaxed protograph representation. Each protograph entry is interpreted as the probability that the corresponding element is equal to one. The loss function is based on DE bit error rate (BER) performance and can be evaluated directly for relaxed protographs. To justify this relaxation, we associate the relaxed representation with an ensemble of binary protographs and show that the proposed relaxed DE gives the ensemble-averaged DE performance. The resulting optimization procedure is fully autonomous and uses standard GD methods. Owing to deterministic DE evaluation and informative gradients, the proposed approach provides fast and reliable convergence. Numerical experiments for the min-sum decoder show that the optimized protographs outperform 5G LDPC codes with the same protograph dimensions.

</details>


### [126] [Locality of Curve-Decoding and Improved Proximity Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08516)
*Rohan Goyal,Venkatesan Guruswami,Yihang Sun,Mary Wootters*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文改进了随机码族的 proximity gap 参数，使随机线性码、随机评价点 Reed-Solomon 码和 Gallager LDPC 随机集成达到与 subspace design codes 相匹配的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作已为多类码建立近最优 proximity gaps，但随机码族的参数劣于 subspace design codes，且随次数 ℓ 增大而退化；本文旨在消除这一差距。

Method: 作者将 LCL（Local Coordinate-wise Linear）性质框架扩展为 row-span constrained 版本，把可推出 proximity gaps 的 curve-decodability 直接表述为 row-span constrained LCL 性质，并通过黑盒迁移从 subspace design codes 推导随机码族结果。

Result: 随机线性码、随机评价点 Reed-Solomon 码和 Gallager LDPC 集成的 proximity gap 参数提升到与 Goyal 和 Guruswami 对 subspace design codes 得到的参数一致；未来 subspace design codes 的改进也可自动迁移到这些随机集成。

Conclusion: 通过更精确地刻画 curve-decodability，本文避免了先前使用代理性质带来的参数损失，建立了 subspace design codes 到多种随机码族的通用转移机制。

Abstract: Proximity gaps are a property of error correcting codes that arise in the study of Interactive Oracle Proofs (IOPs) and Succinct Non-interactive Arguments of Zero Knowledge (SNARKs). Recent work of Goyal and Guruswami has established near-optimal proximity gaps for many families of codes, including subspace design codes, as well as random ensembles like random linear codes, Reed-Solomon codes with random evaluation points, and Gallager's ensemble of LDPC codes (Goyal & Guruswami, 2025). However, the parameters for these latter randomized ensembles are worse than the parameters for subspace design codes, and degrade as the degree ell increases.
  In this work, we obtain improved proximity gaps for random ensembles of codes, including random linear codes, Reed-Solomon codes with random evaluation points, and Gallager's ensemble. Quantitatively, our results for these random ensembles match the results that Goyal and Guruswami attained for subspace design codes. In fact, our techniques are a black-box transference from subspace design codes: any progress on subspace design codes will automatically lead to analogous progress for these random ensembles. To obtain our results, we extend the Local Coordinate-wise Linear (LCL) property framework developed by Levi, Mosheiff, and Shagrithaya and by Brakensiek, Chen, Dhar, and Zhang to a \textit{row-span constrained} version (Levi, Mosheiff & Shagrithaya, 2025; Brakensiek, Chen, Dhar & Zhang, 2025). This allows us to cast \textit{curve-decodability} -- a property that implies proximity gaps -- directly as a row-span constrained LCL property, and make use of that machinery. In contrast, because curve-decodability is not obviously a vanilla LCL property, prior work had worked with a proxy property instead, leading to the aforementioned parameter losses.

</details>


### [127] [On the Convergence of Belief Propagation for Multipath Data Association in Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08521)
*Kuilong Yang,Zengfu Wang,Hua Lan,Jing Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了多径数据关联（MPDA）中信念传播（BP）更新的收敛性，并通过仿真展示其在精度与效率之间的良好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BP在数据关联中的收敛性分析主要针对目标与量测之间的二元对应关系，即每个目标每次扫描最多产生一个量测；而MPDA涉及目标、传播路径和量测之间的三元对应关系，尚缺乏完整的收敛性证明。

Method: 作者针对MPDA场景下的BP更新过程进行理论分析，证明其收敛到唯一不动点；同时通过仿真实验考察BP在MPDA中的收敛行为，并与多检测多假设跟踪器的单扫描和双扫描变体进行比较。

Result: 理论上建立了MPDA中BP更新的收敛性与唯一不动点性质；实验上显示BP在MPDA中具有稳定的收敛表现，并相较对比方法呈现更优的精度—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作补足了MPDA中BP收敛性理论空白，表明BP可作为多径数据关联目标跟踪中的可靠且高效推断方法。

Abstract: Belief propagation (BP) is widely used for data association (DA) in target tracking. Existing convergence analyses of BP for DA address only the two-way correspondence between targets and measurements, where each target generates at most one measurement per scan. Multipath DA (MPDA) allows a single target to produce multiple measurements via distinct propagation paths, creating a three-way correspondence among targets, paths, and measurements, for which a complete convergence proof has not yet been provided. We provide such a proof for the BP updates in MPDA, establishing convergence to a unique fixed point. Simulations illustrate the convergence behavior of BP in MPDA and demonstrate a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off relative to both single-scan and two-scan variants of the multiple-detection multiple-hypothesis tracker.

</details>


### [128] [New sharp inequalities involving non-relative, relative and cross informational functionals with some remarkable minimizers of generalized Gaussian and Beta types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08599)
*Razvan Gabriel Iagar,David Puertas-Centeno*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在相对信息论框架下，基于Stam型不等式、矩-熵型不等式以及一个结合Rényi熵、Rényi散度与Rényi交叉熵的新近不等式，推导出若干新的尖锐信息不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是扩展经典信息不等式到Rényi熵与相对量框架，建立熵功率、Fisher信息、交叉熵和矩泛函之间更精细且达到最优常数的关系，并刻画等号情形。

Method: 作者利用相对框架中的Stam-like与moment-entropy-like不等式，以及Rényi熵、Rényi散度和Rényi交叉熵之间的混合不等式，推导三类结果：连接Rényi熵功率、尺度不变相对Fisher信息与Rényi交叉熵的Stam型不等式；仅含Fisher型信息量的不等式；以及仅含非相对、相对与交叉类型矩泛函的不等式。

Result: 得到多条新的尖锐信息不等式。Stam型不等式在若干情形下由高斯分布对或拉伸高斯分布对达到等号；矩型不等式的极小化分布为广义Beta分布的概率密度。

Conclusion: 该工作系统强化了Rényi信息论中的相对不等式体系，证明相关不等式具有尖锐性，并明确了最优分布结构，为熵、散度、Fisher信息与矩泛函之间的统一分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Several new and sharp informational inequalities are derived as a byproduct of Stam-like and moment-entropy-like inequalities in the relative framework and a recently established inequality mixing the Rényi entropy, the Rényi divergence and the Rényi cross entropy of suitable probability density functions. More precisely, we obtain a Stam-like inequality connecting the Rényi entropy power, the recently introduced scaling-invariant relative Fisher information and the Rényi cross entropy. Furthermore, we derive an inequality involving only Fisher-like informational measures and another inequality involving only moment-like functionals of non-relative, relative and cross types, respectively. All the inequalities are sharp. The minimizers of the Stam-like inequality are, in certain cases, pairs of Gaussian or stretched Gaussian probability densities; in contrast, each minimizer of the moment-like inequality is the probability density of the generalized Beta distribution.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向企业的主动式智能体框架，通过动态上下文图持续建模企业实体、关系与状态变化，并主动向员工推送高价值信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 与智能体框架主要是被动响应用户查询，难以在用户意识到问题之前提供信息；企业场景需要能提前发现变化、判断重要性并主动提示的系统，以提升生产效率。

Method: 构建 Context Graph 作为实时关系数据结构，表示企业实体、关系和状态转移；在其上设计 Delta Detection Engine 监测状态变化，Proactivity Scorer 根据紧急性、相关性和角色匹配度排序候选洞察，Surfacing Layer 借助 LLM 生成带依据的通知；并给出统一评分函数和基于 NetworkX 与 Anthropic Claude API 的端到端实现。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道卫生三个企业案例中，方法达到 Precision@5=0.83，误报率为0.11，并将平均信息浮现时间从反应式基线的47分钟降低到30秒以内。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图的主动式企业智能体能够比传统被动 RAG/Agent 更快、更准确地发现并推送可执行信息，显示出改善企业工作流效率的潜力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [130] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由AI驱动的自然语言交互工具，整合GTAP经济模型与APSIM生物物理模型，用于分析农业供应链冲击及其跨领域影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链受生物物理系统与经济系统耦合影响，容易受到气候、产量、市场等扰动冲击。传统分析往往割裂经济与生物物理维度，且模型使用门槛较高，限制了政策制定者和市场参与者对复杂供应链风险的快速评估。

Method: 研究开发一个AI辅助分析工具，将GTAP等全球贸易/经济均衡模型与APSIM等作物/生物物理模拟模型集成，并通过自然语言查询与响应机制，使用户能够以低门槛方式提出供应链冲击相关问题并获得跨学科分析结果。

Result: 该工具能够支持对农业供应链冲击的综合评估，帮助识别生物物理变化如何传导至经济系统，并以自然语言形式向用户反馈分析结果。摘要未提供具体实验数据、案例结果或量化性能指标。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于构建一个连接经济模型、生物物理模型与自然语言AI交互的决策支持框架，可提升农业供应链风险分析的可访问性与跨学科解释能力，但其有效性仍需更多案例验证和量化评估。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [131] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“对抗性社会认识论”（ASE），用于解释高互动传播环境中行为者如何利用证言、推理、制度认证与默会信任等支架化机制来操纵公共断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概念如认识泡沫、回音室或错误信息扩散不足以解释复杂传播网络中信任机制被策略性利用的方式；作者希望解释可信断言赖以成立的承诺与权利如何被反向利用。

Method: 论文构建一套分析语言，结合认识网络与推论主义语义学来解释断言，并识别破坏支架化公共传播中信任的机制，同时提出审计与纠偏框架。

Result: 作者概述了若干机制：信息扭曲、着色、遗漏、伪造、策略性欠说明，以及对推理链可审计性的削弱；并提出可用于追踪和评估信任破坏的概念工具。

Conclusion: ASE可作为分析密集互动传播环境中信任失效与信息操纵的新框架，有助于审计被破坏的推理链并修复公共沟通中的信任缺口。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [132] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理医疗大语言模型在临床推理中的进展，提出临床能力分级与计算推理模式的双视角框架，并通过多层级基准评测比较18个前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗LLM在临床推理和患者照护中潜力快速增长，但现有研究缺乏将临床能力需求与计算推理机制统一起来的系统化分析，也缺少覆盖不同医学推理层级的评测框架。

Method: 论文采用双视角综述方法：临床侧基于Miller金字塔构建从知识回忆到动态病例管理的五级能力体系；计算侧将演绎、归纳和溯因推理与医学目标和任务对应。同时提出覆盖五个医学推理能力层级的基准数据集，并评测18个先进模型。

Result: 实验显示，医学专用模型在以诊断为中心的任务中表现更优，而通用模型在决策支持和医患对话类任务中更具优势。该结果揭示了模型专门化与通用能力在不同临床任务中的差异。

Conclusion: 该综述认为医疗LLM已在多类推理任务中取得进展，但仍面临数据限制、幻觉、知识 grounding 不足等关键挑战。未来需要发展更安全、可靠且可嵌入临床工作流的医疗LLM系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [133] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“对齐可信性（alignment plausibility）”作为医疗健康领域AI监管构念，用于评估LLM在心理健康支持中的价值规范、训练过程与部署监督是否共同指向安全和患者获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于心理健康支持，但其商业目标常偏向延长用户参与，而有效心理支持有时需要设置边界、制造必要阻力。现有安全措施多为事后反应，侧重显性急性风险，忽视依赖、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念强化等长期隐性风险。

Method: 作者提出三层结构化对齐框架：第一，基于临床实践规范承诺进行明确价值定义；第二，通过训练将这些价值嵌入模型行为；第三，在部署中建立类似临床督导的监督机制，以检测漂移和长期伤害。并类比“生物学可信性”提出“对齐可信性”作为监管论证框架。

Result: 形成了一个用于评估AI健康系统是否值得信任的结构化概念：若系统的价值、训练机制和监督机制彼此一致，并能支持安全、积极的健康结果，则具备较高对齐可信性。

Conclusion: 作者主张，仅靠被动安全响应不足以保障心理健康场景中的LLM安全；应以临床实践安全保障逻辑为参照，通过价值 specification、训练嵌入和部署监督构建可监管、可论证的对齐可信性，以促进患者获益并减少潜在伤害。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [134] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“idiobionics”作为研究智能仿生肢体隐私风险的新方向，强调在提升功能与自主性的同时必须系统识别并缓解潜在隐私威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人假肢/仿生肢体通过传感器与AI控制实现感知、响应和与用户共同适应，但这些能力也可能暴露用户行为、生理或环境信息，从而影响隐私与用户采纳。

Method: 论文以概念性框架构建为主：定义idiobionics，结合相关文献进行理论定位，并给出初步证据与讨论，展示智能仿生肢体设计中可能被利用的对抗性攻击路径。

Result: 研究指出，下一代仿生肢体的传感与控制机制可能形成新的隐私威胁向量；同时整理出一组开放研究问题，面向可穿戴机器人和面向人的自主系统研究者。

Conclusion: 智能仿生肢体的发展需要将隐私风险作为核心议题纳入设计、评估与治理；idiobionics有望推动更安全、可信且易被用户接受的仿生设备发展。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [135] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infinity-Parser2 通过可控合成数据与多任务强化学习，提升端到端文档解析能力，并发布大规模双语数据集与两种模型变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、忠实标注的文档解析语料长期稀缺，限制了复杂文档中版面、表格、公式、图表、化学式与阅读顺序等任务的统一建模与泛化能力。

Method: 构建可扩展的数据合成引擎，结合可控渲染框架和迭代精炼流程，生成 Infinity-Doc2-5M；同时设计可验证的多任务奖励系统，在八类任务上进行联合强化学习，将感知、结构理解与推理统一到单一优化框架中。

Result: 发布 Infinity-Parser2-Flash 与 Infinity-Parser2-Pro。Flash 相比 Infinity-Parser-7B 吞吐提升 3.68 倍；Pro 在 olmOCR-Bench 达到 87.6%，在 ParseBench 达到 74.3%，超过多个对比系统，并在图表、化学公式和文档 VQA 上表现出较强泛化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，大规模可控合成语料结合可验证多任务强化学习，可以有效缓解文档解析标注稀缺问题，并推动统一多模态文档解析模型在效率和精度上的提升。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [136] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 VectorizationLLM：一个基于 Google 开放权重大语言模型、面向 MATLAB 应用计算分析课程的教学辅助模型，结合 RAG 知识库与系统提示，为学生提供概念解释、示例代码、文本与图像辅助，但避免直接给出作业答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在学习智能向量化、时间/波形向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等 MATLAB 相关内容时，往往需要循序渐进的概念讲解、课堂笔记关联示例和编程实践指导。该工作旨在构建一个符合课程教学目标的专用 LLM 助教。

Method: 作者基于 Google 开放权重 LLM 构建专用模型，并采用 RAG（检索增强生成）知识库与系统提示架构，使模型能够调用课程笔记等教学材料生成回答。模型响应可包含代码、文字解释和图像示例，同时通过提示约束避免直接提供问题答案，而是以引导式教学方式辅助学习。

Result: 摘要表明该模型已面向 CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II 课程场景设计，能够围绕 MATLAB 中的向量化、傅里叶分析、微分方程等主题生成教学型解释与多模态示例。摘要未提供定量评估结果、用户实验数据或与通用 LLM 的性能对比。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM 的主要贡献在于将开放权重 LLM、RAG 知识库和课程化提示工程结合，用于构建面向特定工程技术课程的 MATLAB 学习助教。其价值在于增强个性化学习与课堂材料对齐，但仍需进一步通过学习效果、回答准确性和教学安全性评估来验证有效性。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [137] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图网络表示sEMG信号的实时手势识别方法，在8名受试者的Myoband数据上达到99%平均准确率，推理与建图平均耗时48ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进手部假肢和增强现实需要无缝、准确且低延迟的手势控制；前臂sEMG常用于该任务，但需要更有效地表达肌肉激活模式以提升识别性能与实时性。

Method: 将前臂8通道sEMG信号构造成包含肌肉激活模式信息的图网络表示，并基于该图结构设计图神经网络分类算法，用于实时手势识别。

Result: 在8名健康受试者、Myoband 8电极采集的数据上，平均分类准确率达到99%，优于文中所称的现有先进方法；图构建与预测平均总耗时为48ms，运行平台为M1 Pro CPU。

Conclusion: 基于图网络的sEMG表示结合图神经网络能够高精度、低延迟地识别手势，具有用于实时假肢控制和增强现实交互的潜力。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [138] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI架构（LLM、RAG、多智能体Agentic RAG）在精算直通式核保中的应用，提出并评估一个面向小型商业BOP核保的多智能体框架，结果显示其在多步骤推理和信息缺失场景中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算实践越来越需要处理非结构化文档、异构数据源和受监管决策流程。传统自动化、LLM、RAG与多智能体系统各有优势和风险，因此需要评估哪类架构更能满足透明性、可审计性与人在环治理等精算核心要求，尤其是在直通式决策流程中。

Method: 论文构建了一个合成但贴近现实的实验环境，聚焦小型商业Business Owner Policies（BOPs）的直通式核保任务。比较三种管线：单一LLM基线、朴素RAG系统，以及结合定向检索、第三方数据核验、显式多步骤规则评估和反思机制的多智能体Agentic RAG管线。

Result: 多智能体Agentic RAG系统整体表现最佳，尤其在需要多步骤判断和存在信息缺失的场景中提升最大。结构化检索与反思机制帮助模型减少缺乏依据的直通式决策。

Conclusion: Agentic RAG较单一LLM和朴素RAG更适合受监管、高要求的精算核保流程，因为它更能支持透明、可审计、人在环治理和稳健的多步骤决策。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [139] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出反馈操纵正则化（FMR），利用评估性反馈作为纠偏信号，增强模仿学习策略在序贯决策环境中的对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法多依赖多阶段流程，并主要面向语言生成的上下文赌博机设定；对于完整序贯决策环境中，如何将人类示范与反馈作为单阶段离线训练的互补信号仍研究不足。

Method: 提出算法无关的 Feedback Manipulation Regularization（FMR），将评估性反馈引入模仿学习训练过程，作为正则化/纠偏信号以减少策略偏离人类价值或安全约束的行为；同时改造 Safety Gymnasium 环境作为对齐评测测试床。

Result: 在多种模仿学习算法上，FMR 提升了任务能力表现，并在若干设置中最高实现 98% 的不对齐行为减少；在数据有限、 aligned demonstrations 稀缺且示范含无信息噪声时仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FMR 表明，评估性反馈不仅可用于后处理或多阶段偏好优化，也可作为单阶段离线模仿学习中的有效纠偏信号，从而改善序贯决策智能体的对齐性与稳健性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [140] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布尼日利亚工业机械使用与故障的小规模公开数据集及其面向语言模型的链式思维推理样例层，强调真实来源、领域落地与逐行溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲经济体工业机械领域缺乏公开、结构化、可直接用于建模的数据，限制了定量分析以及语言模型在真实数值任务上的训练与评估。

Method: 构建包含89条机器层级记录、28个指标、覆盖2006—2025年尼日利亚制造业与油气部门的数据集；为每条记录提供公共来源和代码本；进一步从稀疏数值记录生成94条包含提示、答案和推理轨迹的CoT样例，并确保提示显式包含指标、子行业、年份与来源。

Result: 相比早期版本，领域落地提示比例从78条中的1条提升到94条中的94条；检索型答案与来源数值完全一致，达到84/84。数据、推理层和逐行来源文件以CC-BY-4.0发布。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个面向尼日利亚工业机械研究和数值推理任务的参考性种子数据集，但规模较小、许多指标仅有单次观测，且多数推理样例偏检索而非复杂多步计算，因此不宜视为大型训练集。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [141] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型的“人格”表示为OCEAN五因素行为特质空间中的位置，并通过低秩适配器对特质进行放大、抑制与组合，实现可测量、可控的人格编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会表现出稳定且反复出现的行为模式，这些模式影响泛化能力与安全性，但现有方法缺乏可靠工具来分解、度量和控制这些行为人格。

Method: 作者采用OCEAN框架刻画模型人格，训练针对单一特质的低秩适配器；使用经人类验证面板校准的LLM裁判、特质相关多选基准和标准能力评测评估效果，并进一步构建无监督心理测量流程从模型输出中提取行为因子。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六个4B到32B模型上，适配器随缩放基本单调地改变目标特质；多个适配器可近似加性组合形成混合人格；中等强度下基本保持标准能力表现。特质轴还会影响下游安全相关行为，如神经质影响挫败反应，宜人性影响迎合倾向。

Conclusion: 人格控制可被建模为在权重空间中学习、缩放与组合行为特质，为人格测量、模型编辑和AI安全之间建立了方法桥梁。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [142] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于视频的孤独症谱系障碍相关自我刺激行为自动检测，比较序列模型与采样率，并评估小样本视频数据增强策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD影响人群规模大，但可扩展的远程行为筛查计算方法仍不足；尤其在小规模临床行为视频数据上，模型架构、时间采样率和数据增强策略缺乏明确实践指导。

Method: 研究在SSBD数据集上提取姿态特征，训练LSTM与GRU，并比较1、5、15、30、45、90帧采样间隔；同时在I3D迁移学习流程中测试10种数据增强策略，并通过消融实验评估各策略边际贡献；此外训练个体化模型，在每个受试者视频的时间切分片段上训练与测试。

Result: LSTM与GRU均超过既有CNN基线（62–76%准确率），在每15帧采样时达到最高准确率：LSTM为97.5%，GRU为98.75%。增强实验中，水平翻转单独使用准确率最高（48.78%）；移除上采样造成最大性能下降，表明其对复杂行为视频增强较关键。个体化模型预测较稳定，平均损失1.84，标准差0.79。

Conclusion: 该研究为数据稀缺临床场景中的视频行为分类提供了具体建议：优先考虑GRU/LSTM等序列模型，采用约15帧采样间隔，并在增强流水线中重视上采样等策略；个体化建模也可能提升稳定性。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames. For the second objective, ten data augmentation strategies were applied to an I3D transfer learning pipeline, with an ablation study quantifying the marginal contribution of each technique. Horizontal flip achieved the highest standalone accuracy (48.78%), while exclusion of upsampling from the augmentation pipeline produced the largest performance degradation, indicating its necessity for complex behavioral video augmentation. A personalized machine learning approach, in which per-subject models were trained and tested on temporally split segments of each video, produced consistent predictions (mean loss 1.84, SD 0.79). These results provide practitioners with concrete guidance on architecture selection, sampling rate, and augmentation strategy for video-based behavioral classification in data-scarce clinical domains.

</details>


### [143] [Agentic Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07984)
*Seokhoon Jeong,Mijung Kim,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentNAS：由大语言模型先生成高质量种子架构并拆解为带命名可替换模块槽的“slotted architecture”，自动形成任务特定搜索空间，再交由传统 NAS 进行组合搜索；在 17 个任务中 11 个达到新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NAS 虽然更高效，但依赖人工设计搜索空间，迁移到新任务时需要大量领域知识和重建成本；LLM 可开放式生成架构，但如何与 NAS 合理分工尚未明确。

Method: 提出连接 LLM 设计与 NAS 搜索的机制：LLM 生成种子网络，并将其分解为包含可替换模块槽的架构骨架，从而自动定义有界、任务相关的搜索空间；实现为三阶段模块化流水线 AgentNAS，并通过消融独立评估各组件贡献。

Result: 在 NAS-Bench-360 和 Unseen NAS 的 17 个多模态任务上评测，覆盖分类、稠密回归、分割和多标签标注；AgentNAS 在 11 个任务上达到新的最优结果，并超过包括任务专家设计在内的已发表基线。消融显示 LLM 种子在多数任务上已优于基线，NAS 进一步通过跨槽组合重组带来增益；结果在三种不同能力水平的 LLM 上保持一致。

Conclusion: LLM 与 NAS 具有互补性：LLM 适合生成高质量任务相关初始设计，NAS 适合在结构化槽位空间中进行系统组合优化。AgentNAS 减少了人工搜索空间工程，提供了一种稳健、可迁移的自动架构设计范式。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) methods have grown increasingly efficient, yet they remain bounded by manually engineered search spaces that require substantial domain expertise and must be rebuilt for every new task. Large language models (LLMs) can generate architectures in an open-ended space, but how to optimally divide the labor between LLM-driven design and NAS-driven search remains unexplored. We propose a mechanism that bridges these two paradigms: an LLM produces a high-quality seed architecture, then decomposes it into a "slotted architecture", a scaffold with named, interchangeable module slots that automatically defines a bounded, task-specific search space for conventional NAS to explore, without manual engineering. We instantiate this mechanism in AgentNAS, a modular three-phase pipeline in which each component's contribution can be measured independently. On 17 tasks spanning classification, dense regression, segmentation, and multi-label tagging across diverse modalities (NAS-Bench-360 and Unseen NAS), AgentNAS establishes a new state of the art on 11 tasks, outperforming published baselines including task-specific expert designs. Ablation studies show that the two search mechanisms are broadly complementary: the LLM-generated seed already surpasses published baselines on the majority of tasks, and NAS delivers additional gains in most cases through combinatorial recombination across slots, a mode of search that independent LLM samples cannot replicate. These patterns hold across three LLMs of different capability levels, confirming that the division of labor is robust. Our code is available at https://github.com/alroimfebruary/AgentNAS.

</details>


### [144] [Concretized Proposition Prompting Resolves Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08018)
*Changhun Lee,Minguk Jeon,Jongkyung Shin,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Concretized Proposition Prompting（CPP），通过显式具体化与问题相关的命题，缓解大语言模型在组合推理与知识准确性之间的矛盾，并在医学等知识密集型任务上显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂问题中常难以同时保持良好的组合推理能力和事实知识可靠性，作者将这一现象定义为 Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy。该问题在需要精确知识支撑的任务中尤为突出，因此需要一种方法将逻辑组织与事实 grounding 更好结合。

Method: 作者提出 CPP 框架，在回答问题前显式生成或具体化与问题相关的命题，使模型能够围绕清晰、事实化的中间命题进行推理，从而连接知识检索式思考与组合式推理。

Result: 实验显示，CPP 能显著提升推理性能，尤其在医学基准测试中效果明显；在更强调演绎推理的数学基准上也具有竞争力。此外，CPP 可扩展到不同基础模型和参数规模。

Conclusion: CPP 被证明是一种基础性提示范式，能够在知识型与组合型方法之间架桥，为逻辑有序且事实 grounded 的推理提供支撑，从而缓解或解决组合性—知识性二分问题。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle to balance compositionality with knowledgeability, a challenge we define as Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy. To address this, we propose Concretized Proposition Prompting (CPP), a framework that explicitly concretizes propositions relevant to questions. The results demonstrate that CPP significantly enhances reasoning performance, particularly in medical benchmarks where precise knowledge is paramount, while being competitive on math benchmarks where deductive reasoning is prioritized. Additional experiments reveal that CPP is scalable to various foundation models and parameter sizes, being a fundamental paradigm that bridges the gap between composition- and knowledge-based approaches. Consequently, CPP resolves the composition-knowledge dichotomy by providing a solid foundation for logically organized and factually grounded reasoning.

</details>
